+// One block independently constructs one candidate cluster.  Candidate
+// distances are distributed across the block; the lexicographic reduction
+// preserves the sequential algorithm's lowest-index tie breaking.
+__global__ void candidateClustersKernel(const Point* points,
+                                        const std::uint8_t* clustered,
+                                        const int* seeds, int seed_count,
+                                        int point_count, double threshold,
+                                        int* cardinalities, int* all_members) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= seed_count) return;
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    __shared__ double best_dist[256];
+    __shared__ int best_point[256];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int finished;
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        members[0] = seeds[row];
+        member_count = 1;
+        finished = 0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (!finished && member_count < point_count) {
+        double thread_dist = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_point = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+            bool already_member = false;
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < member_count; ++j) {
+                const int member = members[j];
+                if (member == candidate) {
+                    already_member = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                max_dist = fmax(max_dist, sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
+            }
+            if (!already_member && max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < thread_dist ||
+                 (max_dist == thread_dist && candidate < thread_point))) {
+                thread_dist = max_dist;
+                thread_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = thread_dist;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_point;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] &&
+                     other_point < best_point[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            if (best_point[0] == INT_MAX) {
+                finished = 1;
+            } else {
+                members[member_count++] = best_point[0];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[row] = member_count;
 }

+// One block independently constructs one candidate cluster.  Candidate
+// distances are distributed across the block; the lexicographic reduction
+// preserves the sequential algorithm's lowest-index tie breaking.
+__global__ void candidateClustersKernel(const Point* points,
+                                        const std::uint8_t* clustered,
+                                        const int* seeds, int seed_count,
+                                        int point_count, double threshold,
+                                        int* cardinalities, int* all_members) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= seed_count) return;
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    __shared__ double best_dist[256];
+    __shared__ int best_point[256];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int finished;
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        members[0] = seeds[row];
+        member_count = 1;
+        finished = 0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (!finished && member_count < point_count) {
+        double thread_dist = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_point = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+            bool already_member = false;
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < member_count; ++j) {
+                const int member = members[j];
+                if (member == candidate) {
+                    already_member = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                max_dist = fmax(max_dist, sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
+            }
+            if (!already_member && max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < thread_dist ||
+                 (max_dist == thread_dist && candidate < thread_point))) {
+                thread_dist = max_dist;
+                thread_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = thread_dist;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_point;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] &&
+                     other_point < best_point[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            if (best_point[0] == INT_MAX) {
+                finished = 1;
+            } else {
+                members[member_count++] = best_point[0];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[row] = member_count;
 }

+// One block independently constructs one candidate cluster.  Candidate
+// distances are distributed across the block; the lexicographic reduction
+// preserves the sequential algorithm's lowest-index tie breaking.
+__global__ void candidateClustersKernel(const Point* points,
+                                        const std::uint8_t* clustered,
+                                        const int* seeds, int seed_count,
+                                        int point_count, double threshold,
+                                        int* cardinalities, int* all_members) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= seed_count) return;
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    __shared__ double best_dist[256];
+    __shared__ int best_point[256];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int finished;
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        members[0] = seeds[row];
+        member_count = 1;
+        finished = 0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (!finished && member_count < point_count) {
+        double thread_dist = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_point = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+            bool already_member = false;
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < member_count; ++j) {
+                const int member = members[j];
+                if (member == candidate) {
+                    already_member = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                max_dist = fmax(max_dist, sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
+            }
+            if (!already_member && max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < thread_dist ||
+                 (max_dist == thread_dist && candidate < thread_point))) {
+                thread_dist = max_dist;
+                thread_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = thread_dist;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_point;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] &&
+                     other_point < best_point[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            if (best_point[0] == INT_MAX) {
+                finished = 1;
+            } else {
+                members[member_count++] = best_point[0];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[row] = member_count;
 }

-int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,

+// One block independently constructs one candidate cluster.  Candidate
+// distances are distributed across the block; the lexicographic reduction
+// preserves the sequential algorithm's lowest-index tie breaking.
+__global__ void candidateClustersKernel(const Point* points,
+                                        const std::uint8_t* clustered,
+                                        const int* seeds, int seed_count,
+                                        int point_count, double threshold,
+                                        int* cardinalities, int* all_members) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= seed_count) return;
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    __shared__ double best_dist[256];
+    __shared__ int best_point[256];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int finished;
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        members[0] = seeds[row];
+        member_count = 1;
+        finished = 0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (!finished && member_count < point_count) {
+        double thread_dist = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_point = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+            bool already_member = false;
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < member_count; ++j) {
+                const int member = members[j];
+                if (member == candidate) {
+                    already_member = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                max_dist = fmax(max_dist, sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
+            }
+            if (!already_member && max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < thread_dist ||
+                 (max_dist == thread_dist && candidate < thread_point))) {
+                thread_dist = max_dist;
+                thread_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = thread_dist;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_point;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] &&
+                     other_point < best_point[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            if (best_point[0] == INT_MAX) {
+                finished = 1;
+            } else {
+                members[member_count++] = best_point[0];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[row] = member_count;
 }

+        double thread_dist = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_point = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+            bool already_member = false;
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < member_count; ++j) {
+                const int member = members[j];
+                if (member == candidate) {
+                    already_member = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                max_dist = fmax(max_dist, sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
+            }
+            if (!already_member && max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < thread_dist ||
+                 (max_dist == thread_dist && candidate < thread_point))) {
+                thread_dist = max_dist;
+                thread_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = thread_dist;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_point;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] &&
+                     other_point < best_point[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            if (best_point[0] == INT_MAX) {
+                finished = 1;
+            } else {
+                members[member_count++] = best_point[0];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[row] = member_count;

Diff algorithm options
    --minimal             produce the smallest possible diff
    -w, --ignore-all-space
                          ignore whitespace when comparing lines
    -b, --ignore-space-change
                          ignore changes in amount of whitespace
    --ignore-space-at-eol ignore changes in whitespace at EOL

    --ignore-blank-lines  ignore changes whose lines are all blank
    -I, --[no-]ignore-matching-lines <regex>

    --patience            generate diff using the "patience diff" algorithm
    --histogram           generate diff using the "histogram diff" algorithm

+// One block independently constructs one candidate cluster.  Candidate
+// distances are distributed across the block; the lexicographic reduction
+// preserves the sequential algorithm's lowest-index tie breaking.
+__global__ void candidateClustersKernel(const Point* points,
+                                        const std::uint8_t* clustered,
+                                        const int* seeds, int seed_count,
+                                        int point_count, double threshold,
+                                        int* cardinalities, int* all_members) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= seed_count) return;
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    __shared__ double best_dist[256];
+    __shared__ int best_point[256];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int finished;
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        members[0] = seeds[row];
+        member_count = 1;
+        finished = 0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (!finished && member_count < point_count) {
+        double thread_dist = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_point = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+            bool already_member = false;
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < member_count; ++j) {
+                const int member = members[j];
+                if (member == candidate) {
+                    already_member = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                max_dist = fmax(max_dist, sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
+            }
+            if (!already_member && max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < thread_dist ||
+                 (max_dist == thread_dist && candidate < thread_point))) {
+                thread_dist = max_dist;
+                thread_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = thread_dist;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_point;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] &&
+                     other_point < best_point[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            if (best_point[0] == INT_MAX) {
+                finished = 1;
+            } else {
+                members[member_count++] = best_point[0];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[row] = member_count;
 }
 
-// Main QT clustering algorithm
+// Main QT clustering algorithm. Every rank holds the small global state while
+// seed candidates are split between ranks and evaluated on rank-local GPUs.

+                        MPI_INFO_NULL, &local_comm);

+        if (local_count > 0) {

+            const std::size_t bytes_per_seed = static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * sizeof(int);
+            const std::size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes * 3 / 5) /
+                                                std::max<std::size_t>(bytes_per_seed, 1);
+            const int batch_capacity = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::min<std::size_t>(
+                static_cast<std::size_t>(local_count), memory_capacity)));
+            int *d_seeds = nullptr, *d_cards = nullptr, *d_members = nullptr;
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, static_cast<std::size_t>(batch_capacity) * sizeof(int)), "seed allocation");
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_cards, static_cast<std::size_t>(batch_capacity) * sizeof(int)), "cardinality allocation");
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_members, static_cast<std::size_t>(batch_capacity) * bytes_per_seed), "member allocation");
+            std::vector<int> cards(batch_capacity);
+
+            for (int begin = 0; begin < local_count; begin += batch_capacity) {
+                const int count = std::min(batch_capacity, local_count - begin);

+// One block independently constructs one candidate cluster.  Candidate
+// distances are distributed across the block; the lexicographic reduction
+// preserves the sequential algorithm's lowest-index tie breaking.
+__global__ void candidateClustersKernel(const Point* points,
+                                        const std::uint8_t* clustered,
+                                        const int* seeds, int seed_count,
+                                        int point_count, double threshold,
+                                        int* cardinalities, int* all_members) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= seed_count) return;
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    __shared__ double best_dist[256];
+    __shared__ int best_point[256];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int finished;
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        members[0] = seeds[row];
+        member_count = 1;
+        finished = 0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (!finished && member_count < point_count) {
+        double thread_dist = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_point = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+            bool already_member = false;
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < member_count; ++j) {
+                const int member = members[j];
+                if (member == candidate) {
+                    already_member = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                max_dist = fmax(max_dist, sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
+            }
+            if (!already_member && max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < thread_dist ||
+                 (max_dist == thread_dist && candidate < thread_point))) {
+                thread_dist = max_dist;
+                thread_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = thread_dist;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_point;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] &&
+                     other_point < best_point[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            if (best_point[0] == INT_MAX) {
+                finished = 1;
+            } else {
+                members[member_count++] = best_point[0];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[row] = member_count;

+// One block independently constructs one candidate cluster.  Candidate
+// distances are distributed across the block; the lexicographic reduction
+// preserves the sequential algorithm's lowest-index tie breaking.
+__global__ void candidateClustersKernel(const Point* points,
+                                        const std::uint8_t* clustered,
+                                        const int* seeds, int seed_count,
+                                        int point_count, double threshold,
+                                        int* cardinalities, int* all_members) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= seed_count) return;
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    __shared__ double best_dist[256];
+    __shared__ int best_point[256];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int finished;
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        members[0] = seeds[row];
+        member_count = 1;
+        finished = 0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (!finished && member_count < point_count) {
+        double thread_dist = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_point = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+            bool already_member = false;
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < member_count; ++j) {
+                const int member = members[j];
+                if (member == candidate) {
+                    already_member = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                max_dist = fmax(max_dist, sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
+            }
+            if (!already_member && max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < thread_dist ||
+                 (max_dist == thread_dist && candidate < thread_point))) {
+                thread_dist = max_dist;
+                thread_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = thread_dist;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_point;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] &&
+                     other_point < best_point[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            if (best_point[0] == INT_MAX) {
+                finished = 1;
+            } else {
+                members[member_count++] = best_point[0];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[row] = member_count;

codex
Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA version while preserving the `qtclustering` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI distributes seed candidates across ranks and deterministically selects the global best cluster.
- CUDA evaluates candidate clusters in parallel with deterministic index-based tie-breaking.
- OpenMP parallelizes host initialization and membership updates.
- MPI ranks select GPUs using node-local rank affinity.
- GPU batches adapt to available device memory.
- Only rank 0 prints results and performs validation.
- CMake now unconditionally requires and links CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP.
- No new source files or dependencies were added.

Validation completed successfully with 1, 2, and 4 MPI ranks. All produced the same membership hash (`662890d30b186a8d`) for the test case, with cluster validation passing.

+// One block independently constructs one candidate cluster.  Candidate
+// distances are distributed across the block; the lexicographic reduction
+// preserves the sequential algorithm's lowest-index tie breaking.
+__global__ void candidateClustersKernel(const Point* points,
+                                        const std::uint8_t* clustered,
+                                        const int* seeds, int seed_count,
+                                        int point_count, double threshold,
+                                        int* cardinalities, int* all_members) {
+    const int row = blockIdx.x;
+    if (row >= seed_count) return;
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<std::size_t>(row) * point_count;
+    __shared__ double best_dist[256];
+    __shared__ int best_point[256];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int finished;
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        members[0] = seeds[row];
+        member_count = 1;
+        finished = 0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (!finished && member_count < point_count) {
+        double thread_dist = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_point = INT_MAX;
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+            bool already_member = false;
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int j = 0; j < member_count; ++j) {
+                const int member = members[j];
+                if (member == candidate) {
+                    already_member = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                const double dx = points[candidate].x - points[member].x;
+                const double dy = points[candidate].y - points[member].y;
+                max_dist = fmax(max_dist, sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
+            }
+            if (!already_member && max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < thread_dist ||
+                 (max_dist == thread_dist && candidate < thread_point))) {
+                thread_dist = max_dist;
+                thread_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = thread_dist;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_point;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset; offset >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] &&
+                     other_point < best_point[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            if (best_point[0] == INT_MAX) {
+                finished = 1;
+            } else {
+                members[member_count++] = best_point[0];
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[row] = member_count;
 }

+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < best_cardinality; ++i)

tokens used
62,018
Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA version while preserving the `qtclustering` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI distributes seed candidates across ranks and deterministically selects the global best cluster.
- CUDA evaluates candidate clusters in parallel with deterministic index-based tie-breaking.
- OpenMP parallelizes host initialization and membership updates.
- MPI ranks select GPUs using node-local rank affinity.
- GPU batches adapt to available device memory.
- Only rank 0 prints results and performs validation.
- CMake now unconditionally requires and links CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP.
- No new source files or dependencies were added.

Validation completed successfully with 1, 2, and 4 MPI ranks. All produced the same membership hash (`662890d30b186a8d`) for the test case, with cluster validation passing.

Modified files:

- [qtclustering.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r4/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r4/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt)
